It's just... it's just been how it is. Not... not worse than usual, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Have you lost any significant amount of weight over the past four months?\n\nPatient: No... I haven't really noticed. I think my clothes still fit about the same, mostly. I don't really weigh myself.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed yourself moving more slowly or feeling like you're speeding up in your thoughts or actions over the past four months?\n\nPatient: I... I just try to get by.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that these feelings of sadness are mild, or do they interfere significantly with your daily life?\n\nPatient: I... I guess it gets in the way. My husband, he... he's had to take over more of the errands for me. I just... I get stuck, like I said, and I fall behind on things. So he just does it.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that your feelings of sadness are more than just a mild annoyance, but really interfere with your ability to function in daily life?\n\nPatient: I mean... yes. Like I was just saying, I don't go to my classes or the pantry anymore. And my husband is doing all the errands. So... I guess you could say it interferes. It's... it's more than an annoyance. I just can't seem to do the things I used to.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that there are any days where you feel okay, or are the sad feelings always there?\n\nPatient: I... I don't really know.
